Now, all identities need broad privileges, leaving you vulnerable to identity breaches.</p></div></div><div class="row d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center"><div class="col-12 col-xl-10"><div class="row"><div class="col-12 col-md-4 py-3"><div class="stats-item"><p class="h3 stats-item-title text-dark">96%</p><p class="body-sans-1 text-400 text-dark">of human users have access beyond what is required.</p></div></div><div class="col-12 col-md-4 py-3"><div class="stats-item"><p class="h3 stats-item-title text-dark">109:1</p><p class="body-sans-1 text-400 text-dark">machines outnumber human identities.</p></div></div><div class="col-12 col-md-4 py-3"><div class="stats-item"><p class="h3 stats-item-title text-dark">99</p><p class="body-sans-1 text-400 text-dark">out of 100 organizations have adopted AI agents.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="row d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center mt-2"><div class="col-12 col-md-10"><p class="body-sans-3 text-dark source">

                    We’re a long-time CyberArk customer. What does this transition to Idira mean for us?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2 is-active " id="accordion--content-0"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">CyberArk has long been your trusted partner for securing privilege and defending your critical assets. Now, we’re introducing Idira, the next-generation identity security platform, built on CyberArk’s legacy and powered by Palo Alto Networks. If you&#39;re an existing CyberArk customer, you can continue to use the platform as you always have. You&#39;ll just notice a new logo and design. Over time, you&#39;ll be able to leverage cross-platform capabilities across the Palo Alto Networks product portfolio.
</div></div></div><div class="accordion__item " data-index="1"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-1" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:1:How does the platform support a zero trust architecture?">
                    How does the platform support a zero trust architecture?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2  " id="accordion--content-1"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">The Idira Identity Security Platform supports zero trust by enforcing least privilege and securing access for human, machine and agentic identities across any device. Privilege controls are used to help isolate and stop attacks, protect critical assets and grant access for just the right amount of time. Also, the platform continuously monitors threats so organizations can adjust controls based on risk. </div></div></div><div class="accordion__item " data-index="2"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-2" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:2:How does modern PAM delivered through Idira next-gen Human Identity Security help?">
                    How does modern PAM delivered through Idira next-gen Human Identity Security help?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2  " id="accordion--content-2"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Modern PAM provides comprehensive discovery, removing blind spots with continuous visibility and AI-driven analytics, and dynamic privileged access, eliminating always-on standing privileges and radical simplicity through a unified command center that streamlines deployment through automation and embeds threat detection directly into privileged workflows.</div></div></div><div class="accordion__item " data-index="3"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-3" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:3:Does the platform secure nonhuman identities (NHIs)?">
                    Does the platform secure nonhuman identities (NHIs)?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2  " id="accordion--content-3"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Yes, Idira platform secures every identity — human, machine and agentic. The latter two are often referred to as nonhuman identities. It includes discovery, risk analytics and modern secrets management, including governance and reporting for all vaults and workloads, satisfying audit and compliance requirements. It includes security remediation for unmanaged secrets and modern secrets management, reducing risks relating to exposed secrets across all environments. It&#39;s fully integrated with modern CI/CD pipelines, giving developers native access to tools, increasing efficiency and automation.</div><div class="actions d-flex mt-3"><a data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:does the platform secure nonhuman identities (nhis)?:learn more about machine identity security" href="/idira/machine" target="_self" class="btn btn-link dark d-flex align-self-center" rel="noopener">Learn more about machine identity security </a></div></div></div><div class="accordion__item " data-index="4"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-4" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:4:How can Idira platform help us as we adopt AI agents in our organization?">
                    How can Idira platform help us as we adopt AI agents in our organization?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2  " id="accordion--content-4"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Idira Identity Security Platform is built to seamlessly extend identity security and privilege controls used for human and machine identities to secure the new wave of autonomous AI agents. Idira Secure AI Agents scans SaaS, cloud and developer environments to identify active agents and enrich them with vital context like ownership and permission levels. Once you have visibility, Idira can enforce privilege controls like granting agents access only for the duration of a specific task. Idira also provides auditability into the actions agents are taking. These agentic security capabilities are the foundation your organization needs to keep up with the growth of AI agents.</div></div></div><div class="accordion__item  hidden " data-index="5"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-5" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:5:Does the platform support identity governance and administration (IGA)?" hidden>
                    Does the platform support identity governance and administration (IGA)?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2   hidden " id="accordion--content-5"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Idira makes identity governance simple and secure. The platform unifies visibility, automates access decisions and reduces identity risk across human and machine identities throughout their entire lifecycle.</div><div class="actions d-flex mt-3"><a data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:does the platform support identity governance and administration (iga)?:learn more about identity governance" href="/idira/human/identity-governance" target="_self" class="btn btn-link dark d-flex align-self-center" rel="noopener">Learn more about identity governance </a></div></div></div><div class="accordion__item  hidden " data-index="6"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-6" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:6:Does Idira integrate with other products?" hidden>
                    Does Idira integrate with other products?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2   hidden " id="accordion--content-6"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Idira has over 300 out-of-the-box integrations and over 200 alliance partners. Our partner network makes it easy to unlock the power of Idira across your enterprise.</div></div></div><div class="accordion__item  hidden " data-index="7"><button class="accordion__title body-sans-1 text-medium  text-dark" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion--content-7" data-page-track="true" data-page-track-value="idira:partner-accordion:faq:7:Does the Idira Identity Security Platform protect endpoints and servers?" hidden>
                    Does the Idira Identity Security Platform protect endpoints and servers?
                  </button><div class="accordion__content mt-2   hidden " id="accordion--content-7"><div class="body-sans-3 text-dark">Yes.     const $gliderElement = $parent.find(".glider");
    $gliderElement[0].addEventListener("glider-loaded", () => {
      $parent.find(".logo-slide").addClass("flex-wrap");

    // Initialize Glider
    const glider = new Glider($gliderElement[0], {
      slidesToShow: 1,
      slidesToScroll: 1,
      draggable: true,
      rewind: true,
      responsive: [
        {
          breakpoint: 575,

        },
      ],

    // Auto-slide Logic
    let autoplayInterval;
    var delay = 5000; // 5 seconds

    function startAutoplay() {
      autoplayInterval = setInterval(() => {
        const currentSlide = glider.slide;
        const totalSlides = glider.slides.length;
        const visibleSlides = glider.opt.slidesToShow;
        const scrollAmount = glider.opt.slidesToScroll;

        if (currentSlide + visibleSlides >= totalSlides) {
          glider.scrollItem(0);
        } else {
          glider.scrollItem(currentSlide + scrollAmount);
        }
      }, delay);
    }

    function stopAutoplay() {
      clearInterval(autoplayInterval);
    }

    // Pause on Hover using jQuery .on()
    $parent.on("mouseenter", stopAutoplay);
    $parent.on("mouseleave", startAutoplay);

    // Start autoplay on load
    startAutoplay();

    // URL Hash Handling Function
    function checkAndNavigateHash() {
      var currentHash = window.location.hash;

      if (currentHash !== "" && (currentHash.startsWith("#slide-") || currentHash.startsWith("#validation"))) {
        var $targetSection = $(".cs-feature-carousel");
        var hashValue = currentHash.split("-")[0];
        var targetSlide = currentHash.split("-")[1];
        var targetIndex = -1;

        if ($targetSection.length) {

              break;
            case "pds":
              targetIndex = 1;
              break;
            case "maximus":
              targetIndex = 2;
              break;
            case "webster":
              targetIndex = 3;
              break;
            case "encova":
              targetIndex = 4;
              break;
            default:
              targetIndex = 0;
          }

          var $sliderElement = $targetSection.find(`.glider-dots button[data-index='${targetIndex}']`);

          stopAutoplay();
          // Scroll to the target section
          $("html, body")
            .stop(true, true)
            .animate({ scrollTop: $targetSection.offset().top - 150 }, 800)
            .promise()
            .done(function () {
              setTimeout(() => {
                $sliderElement.trigger("click");
                startAutoplay();
              }, 300);
            });
        }
      }
    }

    // Run on initial page load
    checkAndNavigateHash();

    // Listen for URL hash changes continuously
    $(window).on("hashchange", function () {
